TOKEN-DRIFT LINT (run BEFORE the Playwright steps):
For the files changed in this craft run, grep for raw values that should be tokens:
- Raw hex codes: pattern `#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}` — should be 0 unless inside a comment or DESIGN.md token definition.
- Raw px values outside media queries: pattern `\d+px` — should be 0 outside `@media` rules.
- Raw font-family literals: pattern `font-family:\s*['"]` — should be 0; fonts come from DESIGN.md tokens.
- Raw spacing literals: pattern `(margin|padding):\s*\d` — should match the spacing scale in DESIGN.md.
For each match, confirm the value comes from a token in DESIGN.md. If a hardcoded value isn't backed by a token, STOP — either add the token to DESIGN.md (with my approval) or replace with the existing token. Drift accumulates page by page; catching it here is cheaper than Phase 8.

Step 2 — Iterate

Step 2 source instructions
Step 2 — Iterate (only if it's "almost right but not quite")


Look at the page in the browser. If something feels off, run a single named-axis refine command instead of describing the problem in prose. Each axis has one command. If a command isn't recognised by your skill version, drop back to /impeccable craft with a refinement prompt — the named-axis commands are listed in the Impeccable docs but specific availability may vary by skill version.



If the page looks… | Run |

Generic, like every SaaS | /impeccable bolder email-templates |

Too loud, too many colours | /impeccable quieter email-templates |

Grey, lifeless, no identity | /impeccable colorize email-templates |

Fonts feel default or inconsistent | /impeccable typeset email-templates |

Spacing is off, things feel cramped | /impeccable layout email-templates |

Static, jumpy, no motion | /impeccable animate email-templates |

Functional but cold | /impeccable delight email-templates |

Too much on the page | /impeccable distill email-templates |



If you can SEE that something is wrong but cannot put it into words — run /impeccable live. It opens a browser tool: click the element you want to change, get 3 hot-swapped variants, accept the one that feels right. The accepted variant is written back to source automatically. This is the right command when you're not a designer and can't name the issue.

Note — Live Mode reads DESIGN.md and PRODUCT.md from your project root for on-brand variants. DESIGN.json (the sidecar file /impeccable document generated alongside DESIGN.md in Phase 4) sits at project root and powers Live Mode's design panel. Keep both at project root.


/impeccable live


One iteration rule — never chain refine commands without looking in between. Run one, look, decide whether another is needed. Chained refines undo each other.

TOKEN-DRIFT LINT (run BEFORE the Playwright steps):
For the files changed in this craft run, grep for raw values that should be tokens:
- Raw hex codes: pattern `#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}` — should be 0 unless inside a comment or DESIGN.md token definition.
- Raw px values outside media queries: pattern `\d+px` — should be 0 outside `@media` rules.
- Raw font-family literals: pattern `font-family:\s*['"]` — should be 0; fonts come from DESIGN.md tokens.
- Raw spacing literals: pattern `(margin|padding):\s*\d` — should match the spacing scale in DESIGN.md.
For each match, confirm the value comes from a token in DESIGN.md. If a hardcoded value isn't backed by a token, STOP — either add the token to DESIGN.md (with my approval) or replace with the existing token. Drift accumulates page by page; catching it here is cheaper than Phase 8.

Functional smoke test checklist
🔬 Functional smoke test before commit (per page) — visual approval is not functional approval. Before Step 7 commits this page, run through the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest manually:

- ☐ Click every form, button, and link the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest names. Each one must do what it did before the redesign.

- ☐ Submit one form successfully — open Network tab, verify the request hit the same endpoint as in the BASELINE notes.

- ☐ Test keyboard navigation: Tab order is sensible, Enter/Space activates buttons, Esc closes any open modal.

- ☐ Verify any JS hook the brief flagged (data attributes, event listeners, third-party callbacks) still fires.

- ☐ Verify analytics events still fire — check the Network tab for the event calls listed in the Feature Preservation Manifest's Analytics events section. Internal admin events (audit trails, click tracking) silently lose data if a redesign drops them — do not skip this check.


If any of these fail: do NOT commit. Open the escape valves block above; failure mode 1 (re-run craft with corrections) is usually the right path.

After approval — per-page rollback path depends on which Phase 0a branch you chose:

DIFF REVIEW BEFORE COMMIT (required):
Run `git diff --stat` and compare the changed-files list against /redesign/per-page-scope/email-templates-scope.md (the scope contract written before craft ran). For any file changed that isn't in the scope's "Files to edit" list — STOP and review. Either revert the unscoped change or update the scope file with my approval. This is the scope-creep guard. Run `git diff` (full) on the most recent change and confirm nothing unexpected appears before staging.

BRANCH A (git was set up):
Read /redesign/per-page-scope/email-templates-scope.md. For each file in the "Files to edit" section, run `git add [file]` explicitly. Do NOT use `git add .` or `git add -A`.
git commit -m "redesign: email-templates"
After staging and before commit, run `git diff --cached` and confirm only files from the scope file are staged. If any unscoped file is staged, unstage it (`git reset HEAD [file]`) before committing.

BRANCH B with git init (you said yes to git during Phase 0a):
Same as Branch A — git add + commit per page.

BRANCH B without git init (zip backup only):
After each approved page, make an incremental zip:
  ../redesign-progress-[YYYYMMDD]-email-templates.zip
Stash it next to the original pre-redesign-backup zip from Phase 0a. This is your per-page rollback point.

BRANCH C (CMS / staging):
After each approved page, save the staging snapshot per your host's flow:
- WordPress (WP Engine / Kinsta / SiteGround): create a manual Backup Point named "redesign-email-templates"
- Webflow: publish to staging only — DON'T publish to production yet
- Shopify: the duplicate theme stays the rollback target; do not Publish until Phase 7 sign-off
- Wix / Squarespace: no native versioning — keep the duplicated/unpublished page set as your rollback target
Log each snapshot's identifier in /redesign/RECIPE-PROGRESS.md so you can name it for rollback later.

Then move to the next brief. Use /redesign/RECIPE-PROGRESS.md to track which pages are done.

Rollback options:
- Brief is intact — re-run /impeccable craft with it in a fresh session
- baseline screenshots in /redesign/baseline/
- DESIGN.md is unchanged — your design direction is not lost regardless of what happens to a single page
- Branch A or B-with-git: git revert HEAD (single page) or git reset --hard redesign-baseline (all)
- Branch B without git: extract the relevant per-page progress zip
- Branch C: restore the named Backup Point / republish the duplicate theme / revert to the last unpublished snapshot per host

Step 2 — Iterate (only if it's "almost right but not quite")

If the page looks… | Run |

Generic, like every SaaS | /impeccable bolder emails |

Functional but cold | /impeccable delight emails |

Too much on the page | /impeccable distill emails |



If you can SEE that something is wrong but cannot put it into words — run /impeccable live. It opens a browser tool: click the element you want to change, get 3 hot-swapped variants, accept the one that feels right. The accepted variant is written back to source automatically. This is the right command when you're not a designer and can't name the issue.

Note — Live Mode reads DESIGN.md and PRODUCT.md from your project root for on-brand variants. DESIGN.json (the sidecar file /impeccable document generated alongside DESIGN.md in Phase 4) sits at project root and powers Live Mode's design panel. Keep both at project root.

One iteration rule — never chain refine commands without looking in between. Run one, look, decide whether another is needed. Chained refines undo each other.

TOKEN-DRIFT LINT (run BEFORE the Playwright steps):
For the files changed in this craft run, grep for raw values that should be tokens:
- Raw hex codes: pattern `#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}` — should be 0 unless inside a comment or DESIGN.md token definition.
- Raw px values outside media queries: pattern `\d+px` — should be 0 outside `@media` rules.
- Raw font-family literals: pattern `font-family:\s*['"]` — should be 0; fonts come from DESIGN.md tokens.
- Raw spacing literals: pattern `(margin|padding):\s*\d` — should match the spacing scale in DESIGN.md.
For each match, confirm the value comes from a token in DESIGN.md. If a hardcoded value isn't backed by a token, STOP — either add the token to DESIGN.md (with my approval) or replace with the existing token. Drift accumulates page by page; catching it here is cheaper than Phase 8.

ROUTE FINDINGS:
- P0 audit findings → STOP. Fix before this page is approved. Do not start Step 8 (handoff).
- P1 audit findings → tag for Phase 7 gauntlet (do not fix per-page).
- P2/P3 → list in PER-PAGE-SCORES.md, leave for next sprint.
- Critique AI-slop verdict regressed vs baseline → STOP. Re-run /impeccable bolder or /impeccable distill on the page, then re-critique.

Tell me the per-page scores and the P0 list before Step 8.
Functional smoke test checklist
🔬 Functional smoke test before commit (per page) — visual approval is not functional approval. Before Step 7 commits this page, run through the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest manually:

- ☐ Click every form, button, and link the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest names. Each one must do what it did before the redesign.

- ☐ Submit one form successfully — open Network tab, verify the request hit the same endpoint as in the BASELINE notes.

- ☐ Test keyboard navigation: Tab order is sensible, Enter/Space activates buttons, Esc closes any open modal.

- ☐ Verify any JS hook the brief flagged (data attributes, event listeners, third-party callbacks) still fires.

- ☐ Verify analytics events still fire — check the Network tab for the event calls listed in the Feature Preservation Manifest's Analytics events section. Internal admin events (audit trails, click tracking) silently lose data if a redesign drops them — do not skip this check.


If any of these fail: do NOT commit. Open the escape valves block above; failure mode 1 (re-run craft with corrections) is usually the right path.

Step 8 — Handoff / Commit

Step 8 handoff and commit prompt
Confirm the dev server is still running.
Tell me the EXACT URL of the redesigned page (e.g. http://localhost:3000/dashboard).
Send me the screenshots and the audit + critique scores.
Note any deviations from the brief.

Wait while I open the URL on my own devices, click around, and approve. Do NOT start the next page until I say "approved".

After approval — per-page rollback path depends on which Phase 0a branch you chose:

DIFF REVIEW BEFORE COMMIT (required):
Run `git diff --stat` and compare the changed-files list against /redesign/per-page-scope/emails-scope.md (the scope contract written before craft ran). For any file changed that isn't in the scope's "Files to edit" list — STOP and review. Either revert the unscoped change or update the scope file with my approval. This is the scope-creep guard. Run `git diff` (full) on the most recent change and confirm nothing unexpected appears before staging.

BRANCH A (git was set up):
Read /redesign/per-page-scope/emails-scope.md. For each file in the "Files to edit" section, run `git add [file]` explicitly. Do NOT use `git add .` or `git add -A`.
git commit -m "redesign: emails"
After staging and before commit, run `git diff --cached` and confirm only files from the scope file are staged. If any unscoped file is staged, unstage it (`git reset HEAD [file]`) before committing.

BRANCH B with git init (you said yes to git during Phase 0a):
Same as Branch A — git add + commit per page.

BRANCH B without git init (zip backup only):
After each approved page, make an incremental zip:
  ../redesign-progress-[YYYYMMDD]-emails.zip
Stash it next to the original pre-redesign-backup zip from Phase 0a. This is your per-page rollback point.

BRANCH C (CMS / staging):
After each approved page, save the staging snapshot per your host's flow:
- WordPress (WP Engine / Kinsta / SiteGround): create a manual Backup Point named "redesign-emails"
- Webflow: publish to staging only — DON'T publish to production yet
- Shopify: the duplicate theme stays the rollback target; do not Publish until Phase 7 sign-off
- Wix / Squarespace: no native versioning — keep the duplicated/unpublished page set as your rollback target
Log each snapshot's identifier in /redesign/RECIPE-PROGRESS.md so you can name it for rollback later.

Then move to the next brief. Use /redesign/RECIPE-PROGRESS.md to track which pages are done.

Rollback options:
- Brief is intact — re-run /impeccable craft with it in a fresh session
- baseline screenshots in /redesign/baseline/
- DESIGN.md is unchanged — your design direction is not lost regardless of what happens to a single page
- Branch A or B-with-git: git revert HEAD (single page) or git reset --hard redesign-baseline (all)
- Branch B without git: extract the relevant per-page progress zip
- Branch C: restore the named Backup Point / republish the duplicate theme / revert to the last unpublished snapshot per host

Escape Valves

Failure 1 — discard and re-run craft with corrections
The page craft just produced is wrong. Discard the changes (Branch A or B-with-git: git checkout -- [page-files]; Branch B without git: restore from the per-page progress zip; Branch C: revert the staging snapshot for this page).

Re-read /redesign/briefs/emails-brief.md, especially:
- The "Feature Preservation Manifest" — what must NOT change
- The "States" section — empty/loading/error coverage
- The "## Copy" section

Then re-run /impeccable craft redesign of admin page emails WITH these explicit corrections at the top of your message: [list the specific things craft got wrong — "header was deleted, must stay", "submit button regressed to default styling", etc.].

Wait for me to approve the new craft output before moving on.
Failure 2 — edit the brief, re-run craft against the updated brief
The brief at /redesign/briefs/emails-brief.md is wrong about [specific section — Purpose / States / Copy / etc.].

STEP 1 — STOP the current craft if it's still running.
STEP 2 — Edit /redesign/briefs/emails-brief.md directly to fix the wrong section. Save the file.
STEP 3 — Discard the in-progress build (same revert path as Failure 1).
STEP 4 — Re-run /impeccable craft redesign of admin page emails. When craft's internal shape phase asks discovery questions, point it at the UPDATED brief — quote each section back to me as confirmation.
STEP 5 — Tell me which brief section was wrong and how the new build differs before approval.
Failure 3 — park this page, continue with the next one
This page is parking. Mark its row in /redesign/IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN.md from [ ] to [~] and add a one-line reason in the file footer ("parked: emails — reason: [specific issue]").

Discard any in-progress build for this page (same revert path as Failure 1).

When you return to a parked page, start fresh — re-read the brief, identify what went wrong last time, and use the escape valve prompts from failure mode 1 or 2 as your entry point.

Then move to the next undone row in IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN.md (skip the parked row). Tell me which page you're moving to, then await "primed — go" against its re-prime prompt.

Never ship stock photography as final.

Step 2 — Iterate (only if it's "almost right but not quite")

If the page looks… | Run |

Generic, like every SaaS | /impeccable bolder operations |

Too loud, too many colours | /impeccable quieter operations |

Grey, lifeless, no identity | /impeccable colorize operations |

Fonts feel default or inconsistent | /impeccable typeset operations |

Spacing is off, things feel cramped | /impeccable layout operations |

Static, jumpy, no motion | /impeccable animate operations |

Functional but cold | /impeccable delight operations |

Too much on the page | /impeccable distill operations |



If you can SEE that something is wrong but cannot put it into words — run /impeccable live. It opens a browser tool: click the element you want to change, get 3 hot-swapped variants, accept the one that feels right. The accepted variant is written back to source automatically. This is the right command when you're not a designer and can't name the issue.

Note — Live Mode reads DESIGN.md and PRODUCT.md from your project root for on-brand variants. DESIGN.json (the sidecar file /impeccable document generated alongside DESIGN.md in Phase 4) sits at project root and powers Live Mode's design panel. Keep both at project root.


/impeccable live


One iteration rule — never chain refine commands without looking in between. Run one, look, decide whether another is needed. Chained refines undo each other.

TOKEN-DRIFT LINT (run BEFORE the Playwright steps):
For the files changed in this craft run, grep for raw values that should be tokens:
- Raw hex codes: pattern `#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}` — should be 0 unless inside a comment or DESIGN.md token definition.
- Raw px values outside media queries: pattern `\d+px` — should be 0 outside `@media` rules.
- Raw font-family literals: pattern `font-family:\s*['"]` — should be 0; fonts come from DESIGN.md tokens.
- Raw spacing literals: pattern `(margin|padding):\s*\d` — should match the spacing scale in DESIGN.md.
For each match, confirm the value comes from a token in DESIGN.md. If a hardcoded value isn't backed by a token, STOP — either add the token to DESIGN.md (with my approval) or replace with the existing token. Drift accumulates page by page; catching it here is cheaper than Phase 8.

CRITICAL — how to handle craft's internal shape discovery: per the docs, /impeccable craft runs /impeccable shape internally as its first phase, and "the discovery phase is non-skippable and that is the point." Don't try to skip discovery — feed it the existing Phase 5 brief instead. When shape asks its discovery questions (Purpose, User, Content, Feeling, Constraints), quote each section from /redesign/briefs/privacy-brief.md verbatim as your answer, but expect shape to expand or adjust them. Confirm each section back to me, accept any expansions shape proposes, then proceed to craft's load-references and build phases. Note: this means shape effectively runs twice per page — once standalone in Phase 5 to produce the reviewable brief, once again here inside craft. The token cost reflects this; the upside is your brief is reviewed and editable before any code is written.

Match DESIGN.md tokens exactly. If the brief conflicts with the codebase, STOP and ask — do not guess.

RESPONSIVE STRATEGY:
 mobile-first: build 375px first, then enhance to 768 → 1440

IMAGE HANDLING (every image you touch):
1. Already optimised (WebP, ≤200KB, descriptive alt) → proceed
2. Can be auto-converted/compressed → do it AND log to /redesign/IMAGES-NEEDED.md
3. Needs a real new photo → keep current, add data-redesign-needs-photo="reason", log to IMAGES-NEEDED.md
Never ship stock photography as final.

BACKEND FAKE MARKER:
If this page renders any FAKE-tagged backend feature from /redesign/backend-plans/ (or per the brief's "Backend FAKE: …" notes), mark the relevant DOM element with `data-backend-fake="[feature-slug]"` (where [feature-slug] matches the corresponding plan filename). This makes the placeholder traceable for Phase 8's extract pass and the 7→8 handoff check. Without the marker, the placeholder is invisible to the deploy gate.

Step 2 — Iterate (only if it's "almost right but not quite")

If the page looks… | Run |

Generic, like every SaaS | /impeccable bolder privacy |

Too loud, too many colours | /impeccable quieter privacy |

Grey, lifeless, no identity | /impeccable colorize privacy |

Spacing is off, things feel cramped | /impeccable layout privacy |

Static, jumpy, no motion | /impeccable animate privacy |

Functional but cold | /impeccable delight privacy |

Too much on the page | /impeccable distill privacy |



If you can SEE that something is wrong but cannot put it into words — run /impeccable live. It opens a browser tool: click the element you want to change, get 3 hot-swapped variants, accept the one that feels right. The accepted variant is written back to source automatically. This is the right command when you're not a designer and can't name the issue.

Note — Live Mode reads DESIGN.md and PRODUCT.md from your project root for on-brand variants. DESIGN.json (the sidecar file /impeccable document generated alongside DESIGN.md in Phase 4) sits at project root and powers Live Mode's design panel. Keep both at project root.


/impeccable live


One iteration rule — never chain refine commands without looking in between. Run one, look, decide whether another is needed. Chained refines undo each other.

Step 6 — Verify

Step 6 verification prompt
SERVER DETECTION — try ports 3000 / 3001 / 5173 / 4321 / 8000 / 8080. State the URL.

TOKEN-DRIFT LINT (run BEFORE the Playwright steps):
For the files changed in this craft run, grep for raw values that should be tokens:
- Raw hex codes: pattern `#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}` — should be 0 unless inside a comment or DESIGN.md token definition.
- Raw px values outside media queries: pattern `\d+px` — should be 0 outside `@media` rules.
- Raw font-family literals: pattern `font-family:\s*['"]` — should be 0; fonts come from DESIGN.md tokens.
- Raw spacing literals: pattern `(margin|padding):\s*\d` — should match the spacing scale in DESIGN.md.
For each match, confirm the value comes from a token in DESIGN.md. If a hardcoded value isn't backed by a token, STOP — either add the token to DESIGN.md (with my approval) or replace with the existing token. Drift accumulates page by page; catching it here is cheaper than Phase 8.

Verification (in this order — your branch's primary viewport first):

VERIFICATION ORDER:

6. Chrome DevTools: console — zero NEW errors vs /redesign/BASELINE-ISSUES.md

- HANDLED — every BLOCKS-REDESIGN plan file this page depends on is built and confirmed working
- STAGED — built on staging only, Phase 7 cross-checks it
- FAKE — using FAKE placeholder, with `data-backend-fake` marker present in DOM
- N-A — no backend dependencies for this page
This field is what the 6→7 handoff checks before Phase 7 opens.

ROUTE FINDINGS:
- P0 audit findings → STOP. Fix before this page is approved. Do not start Step 8 (handoff).
- P1 audit findings → tag for Phase 7 gauntlet (do not fix per-page).
- P2/P3 → list in PER-PAGE-SCORES.md, leave for next sprint.
- Critique AI-slop verdict regressed vs baseline → STOP. Re-run /impeccable bolder or /impeccable distill on the page, then re-critique.

Tell me the per-page scores and the P0 list before Step 8.
Functional smoke test checklist
🔬 Functional smoke test before commit (per page) — visual approval is not functional approval. Before Step 7 commits this page, run through the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest manually:

- ☐ Click every form, button, and link the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest names. Each one must do what it did before the redesign.

- ☐ Submit one form successfully — open Network tab, verify the request hit the same endpoint as in the BASELINE notes.

- ☐ Test keyboard navigation: Tab order is sensible, Enter/Space activates buttons, Esc closes any open modal.

- ☐ Verify any JS hook the brief flagged (data attributes, event listeners, third-party callbacks) still fires.

- ☐ Verify analytics events still fire — check the Network tab for the event calls listed in the Feature Preservation Manifest's Analytics events section. Internal admin events (audit trails, click tracking) silently lose data if a redesign drops them — do not skip this check.


If any of these fail: do NOT commit. Open the escape valves block above; failure mode 1 (re-run craft with corrections) is usually the right path.

Step 8 — Handoff / Commit

Step 8 handoff and commit prompt
Confirm the dev server is still running.
Tell me the EXACT URL of the redesigned page (e.g. http://localhost:3000/dashboard).
Send me the screenshots and the audit + critique scores.
Note any deviations from the brief.

Wait while I open the URL on my own devices, click around, and approve. Do NOT start the next page until I say "approved".

After approval — per-page rollback path depends on which Phase 0a branch you chose:

DIFF REVIEW BEFORE COMMIT (required):
Run `git diff --stat` and compare the changed-files list against /redesign/per-page-scope/privacy-scope.md (the scope contract written before craft ran). For any file changed that isn't in the scope's "Files to edit" list — STOP and review. Either revert the unscoped change or update the scope file with my approval. This is the scope-creep guard. Run `git diff` (full) on the most recent change and confirm nothing unexpected appears before staging.

BRANCH A (git was set up):
Read /redesign/per-page-scope/privacy-scope.md. For each file in the "Files to edit" section, run `git add [file]` explicitly. Do NOT use `git add .` or `git add -A`.
git commit -m "redesign: privacy"
After staging and before commit, run `git diff --cached` and confirm only files from the scope file are staged. If any unscoped file is staged, unstage it (`git reset HEAD [file]`) before committing.

BRANCH B with git init (you said yes to git during Phase 0a):
Same as Branch A — git add + commit per page.

BRANCH B without git init (zip backup only):
After each approved page, make an incremental zip:
  ../redesign-progress-[YYYYMMDD]-privacy.zip
Stash it next to the original pre-redesign-backup zip from Phase 0a. This is your per-page rollback point.

BRANCH C (CMS / staging):
After each approved page, save the staging snapshot per your host's flow:
- WordPress (WP Engine / Kinsta / SiteGround): create a manual Backup Point named "redesign-privacy"
- Webflow: publish to staging only — DON'T publish to production yet
- Shopify: the duplicate theme stays the rollback target; do not Publish until Phase 7 sign-off
- Wix / Squarespace: no native versioning — keep the duplicated/unpublished page set as your rollback target
Log each snapshot's identifier in /redesign/RECIPE-PROGRESS.md so you can name it for rollback later.

Then move to the next brief. Use /redesign/RECIPE-PROGRESS.md to track which pages are done.

Rollback options:
- Brief is intact — re-run /impeccable craft with it in a fresh session
- baseline screenshots in /redesign/baseline/
- DESIGN.md is unchanged — your design direction is not lost regardless of what happens to a single page
- Branch A or B-with-git: git revert HEAD (single page) or git reset --hard redesign-baseline (all)
- Branch B without git: extract the relevant per-page progress zip
- Branch C: restore the named Backup Point / republish the duplicate theme / revert to the last unpublished snapshot per host

Escape Valves

Failure 1 — discard and re-run craft with corrections
The page craft just produced is wrong. Discard the changes (Branch A or B-with-git: git checkout -- [page-files]; Branch B without git: restore from the per-page progress zip; Branch C: revert the staging snapshot for this page).

Re-read /redesign/briefs/privacy-brief.md, especially:
- The "Feature Preservation Manifest" — what must NOT change
- The "States" section — empty/loading/error coverage
- The "## Copy" section

Then re-run /impeccable craft redesign of admin page privacy WITH these explicit corrections at the top of your message: [list the specific things craft got wrong — "header was deleted, must stay", "submit button regressed to default styling", etc.].

Wait for me to approve the new craft output before moving on.
Failure 2 — edit the brief, re-run craft against the updated brief
The brief at /redesign/briefs/privacy-brief.md is wrong about [specific section — Purpose / States / Copy / etc.].

STEP 1 — STOP the current craft if it's still running.
STEP 2 — Edit /redesign/briefs/privacy-brief.md directly to fix the wrong section. Save the file.
STEP 3 — Discard the in-progress build (same revert path as Failure 1).
STEP 4 — Re-run /impeccable craft redesign of admin page privacy. When craft's internal shape phase asks discovery questions, point it at the UPDATED brief — quote each section back to me as confirmation.
STEP 5 — Tell me which brief section was wrong and how the new build differs before approval.
Failure 3 — park this page, continue with the next one
This page is parking. Mark its row in /redesign/IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN.md from [ ] to [~] and add a one-line reason in the file footer ("parked: privacy — reason: [specific issue]").

Discard any in-progress build for this page (same revert path as Failure 1).

When you return to a parked page, start fresh — re-read the brief, identify what went wrong last time, and use the escape valve prompts from failure mode 1 or 2 as your entry point.

Then move to the next undone row in IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN.md (skip the parked row). Tell me which page you're moving to, then await "primed — go" against its re-prime prompt.

If you can SEE that something is wrong but cannot put it into words — run /impeccable live. It opens a browser tool: click the element you want to change, get 3 hot-swapped variants, accept the one that feels right. The accepted variant is written back to source automatically. This is the right command when you're not a designer and can't name the issue.

Note — Live Mode reads DESIGN.md and PRODUCT.md from your project root for on-brand variants. DESIGN.json (the sidecar file /impeccable document generated alongside DESIGN.md in Phase 4) sits at project root and powers Live Mode's design panel. Keep both at project root.

TOKEN-DRIFT LINT (run BEFORE the Playwright steps):
For the files changed in this craft run, grep for raw values that should be tokens:
- Raw hex codes: pattern `#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}` — should be 0 unless inside a comment or DESIGN.md token definition.
- Raw px values outside media queries: pattern `\d+px` — should be 0 outside `@media` rules.
- Raw font-family literals: pattern `font-family:\s*['"]` — should be 0; fonts come from DESIGN.md tokens.
- Raw spacing literals: pattern `(margin|padding):\s*\d` — should match the spacing scale in DESIGN.md.
For each match, confirm the value comes from a token in DESIGN.md. If a hardcoded value isn't backed by a token, STOP — either add the token to DESIGN.md (with my approval) or replace with the existing token. Drift accumulates page by page; catching it here is cheaper than Phase 8.

Functional smoke test checklist
🔬 Functional smoke test before commit (per page) — visual approval is not functional approval. Before Step 7 commits this page, run through the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest manually:

- ☐ Click every form, button, and link the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest names. Each one must do what it did before the redesign.

- ☐ Submit one form successfully — open Network tab, verify the request hit the same endpoint as in the BASELINE notes.

- ☐ Test keyboard navigation: Tab order is sensible, Enter/Space activates buttons, Esc closes any open modal.

- ☐ Verify any JS hook the brief flagged (data attributes, event listeners, third-party callbacks) still fires.

- ☐ Verify analytics events still fire — check the Network tab for the event calls listed in the Feature Preservation Manifest's Analytics events section. Internal admin events (audit trails, click tracking) silently lose data if a redesign drops them — do not skip this check.


If any of these fail: do NOT commit. Open the escape valves block above; failure mode 1 (re-run craft with corrections) is usually the right path.

Step 8 — Handoff / Commit

Step 8 handoff and commit prompt
Confirm the dev server is still running.
Tell me the EXACT URL of the redesigned page (e.g. http://localhost:3000/dashboard).
Send me the screenshots and the audit + critique scores.
Note any deviations from the brief.

Wait while I open the URL on my own devices, click around, and approve. Do NOT start the next page until I say "approved".

After approval — per-page rollback path depends on which Phase 0a branch you chose:

DIFF REVIEW BEFORE COMMIT (required):
Run `git diff --stat` and compare the changed-files list against /redesign/per-page-scope/role-detail-scope.md (the scope contract written before craft ran). For any file changed that isn't in the scope's "Files to edit" list — STOP and review. Either revert the unscoped change or update the scope file with my approval. This is the scope-creep guard. Run `git diff` (full) on the most recent change and confirm nothing unexpected appears before staging.

BRANCH A (git was set up):
Read /redesign/per-page-scope/role-detail-scope.md. For each file in the "Files to edit" section, run `git add [file]` explicitly. Do NOT use `git add .` or `git add -A`.
git commit -m "redesign: role-detail"
After staging and before commit, run `git diff --cached` and confirm only files from the scope file are staged. If any unscoped file is staged, unstage it (`git reset HEAD [file]`) before committing.

BRANCH B with git init (you said yes to git during Phase 0a):
Same as Branch A — git add + commit per page.

BRANCH B without git init (zip backup only):
After each approved page, make an incremental zip:
  ../redesign-progress-[YYYYMMDD]-role-detail.zip
Stash it next to the original pre-redesign-backup zip from Phase 0a. This is your per-page rollback point.

BRANCH C (CMS / staging):
After each approved page, save the staging snapshot per your host's flow:
- WordPress (WP Engine / Kinsta / SiteGround): create a manual Backup Point named "redesign-role-detail"
- Webflow: publish to staging only — DON'T publish to production yet
- Shopify: the duplicate theme stays the rollback target; do not Publish until Phase 7 sign-off
- Wix / Squarespace: no native versioning — keep the duplicated/unpublished page set as your rollback target
Log each snapshot's identifier in /redesign/RECIPE-PROGRESS.md so you can name it for rollback later.

Then move to the next brief. Use /redesign/RECIPE-PROGRESS.md to track which pages are done.

Rollback options:
- Brief is intact — re-run /impeccable craft with it in a fresh session
- baseline screenshots in /redesign/baseline/
- DESIGN.md is unchanged — your design direction is not lost regardless of what happens to a single page
- Branch A or B-with-git: git revert HEAD (single page) or git reset --hard redesign-baseline (all)
- Branch B without git: extract the relevant per-page progress zip
- Branch C: restore the named Backup Point / republish the duplicate theme / revert to the last unpublished snapshot per host

Escape Valves

Failure 1 — discard and re-run craft with corrections
The page craft just produced is wrong. Discard the changes (Branch A or B-with-git: git checkout -- [page-files]; Branch B without git: restore from the per-page progress zip; Branch C: revert the staging snapshot for this page).

Re-read /redesign/briefs/role-detail-brief.md, especially:
- The "Feature Preservation Manifest" — what must NOT change
- The "States" section — empty/loading/error coverage
- The "## Copy" section

Then re-run /impeccable craft redesign of admin page role-detail WITH these explicit corrections at the top of your message: [list the specific things craft got wrong — "header was deleted, must stay", "submit button regressed to default styling", etc.].

Wait for me to approve the new craft output before moving on.
Failure 2 — edit the brief, re-run craft against the updated brief
The brief at /redesign/briefs/role-detail-brief.md is wrong about [specific section — Purpose / States / Copy / etc.].

STEP 1 — STOP the current craft if it's still running.
STEP 2 — Edit /redesign/briefs/role-detail-brief.md directly to fix the wrong section. Save the file.
STEP 3 — Discard the in-progress build (same revert path as Failure 1).
STEP 4 — Re-run /impeccable craft redesign of admin page role-detail. When craft's internal shape phase asks discovery questions, point it at the UPDATED brief — quote each section back to me as confirmation.
STEP 5 — Tell me which brief section was wrong and how the new build differs before approval.
Failure 3 — park this page, continue with the next one
This page is parking. Mark its row in /redesign/IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN.md from [ ] to [~] and add a one-line reason in the file footer ("parked: role-detail — reason: [specific issue]").

Discard any in-progress build for this page (same revert path as Failure 1).

When you return to a parked page, start fresh — re-read the brief, identify what went wrong last time, and use the escape valve prompts from failure mode 1 or 2 as your entry point.

Then move to the next undone row in IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN.md (skip the parked row). Tell me which page you're moving to, then await "primed — go" against its re-prime prompt.

If the page looks… | Run |

Generic, like every SaaS | /impeccable bolder services |

Too loud, too many colours | /impeccable quieter services |

Static, jumpy, no motion | /impeccable animate services |

Functional but cold | /impeccable delight services |

Too much on the page | /impeccable distill services |



If you can SEE that something is wrong but cannot put it into words — run /impeccable live. It opens a browser tool: click the element you want to change, get 3 hot-swapped variants, accept the one that feels right. The accepted variant is written back to source automatically. This is the right command when you're not a designer and can't name the issue.

Note — Live Mode reads DESIGN.md and PRODUCT.md from your project root for on-brand variants. DESIGN.json (the sidecar file /impeccable document generated alongside DESIGN.md in Phase 4) sits at project root and powers Live Mode's design panel. Keep both at project root.


/impeccable live


One iteration rule — never chain refine commands without looking in between. Run one, look, decide whether another is needed. Chained refines undo each other.

If the page looks… | Run |

Generic, like every SaaS | /impeccable bolder staff-availability |

Too loud, too many colours | /impeccable quieter staff-availability |

Static, jumpy, no motion | /impeccable animate staff-availability |

Functional but cold | /impeccable delight staff-availability |

Too much on the page | /impeccable distill staff-availability |



If you can SEE that something is wrong but cannot put it into words — run /impeccable live. It opens a browser tool: click the element you want to change, get 3 hot-swapped variants, accept the one that feels right. The accepted variant is written back to source automatically. This is the right command when you're not a designer and can't name the issue.

Note — Live Mode reads DESIGN.md and PRODUCT.md from your project root for on-brand variants. DESIGN.json (the sidecar file /impeccable document generated alongside DESIGN.md in Phase 4) sits at project root and powers Live Mode's design panel. Keep both at project root.

One iteration rule — never chain refine commands without looking in between. Run one, look, decide whether another is needed. Chained refines undo each other.

For the files changed in this craft run, grep for raw values that should be tokens:
- Raw hex codes: pattern `#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}` — should be 0 unless inside a comment or DESIGN.md token definition.

Functional smoke test checklist
🔬 Functional smoke test before commit (per page) — visual approval is not functional approval. Before Step 7 commits this page, run through the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest manually:

- ☐ Click every form, button, and link the brief's Feature Preservation Manifest names. Each one must do what it did before the redesign.

- ☐ Submit one form successfully — open Network tab, verify the request hit the same endpoint as in the BASELINE notes.

- ☐ Test keyboard navigation: Tab order is sensible, Enter/Space activates buttons, Esc closes any open modal.

- ☐ Verify any JS hook the brief flagged (data attributes, event listeners, third-party callbacks) still fires.

- ☐ Verify analytics events still fire — check the Network tab for the event calls listed in the Feature Preservation Manifest's Analytics events section. Internal admin events (audit trails, click tracking) silently lose data if a redesign drops them — do not skip this check.
